      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.521">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.521 CONSORTIUM REQUIREMENTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Each consortium, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.518#stat.124D.518.1">124D.518, subdivision 1</a>, must meet at least twice per year to develop and amend as necessary an annual consortium agreement signed by all members and filed with the Department of Education that at a minimum includes:</p>
        <p>(1) a description of the members and fiscal agent of the consortium;</p>
        <p>(2) a description of the contributions of each member of the consortium and the process for distributing state aid among the members; and</p>
        <p>(3) the state adult basic education assurances from the annual adult basic education program application.</p>
        <p>As a condition of membership in a consortium, each member must make a documented contribution toward the cost of adult basic education programming, either as a direct financial contribution, or an in-kind contribution.</p>
        <p>(b) Each consortium's designated fiscal agent must:</p>
        <p>(1) collect data from consortium members;</p>
        <p>(2) submit required performance reports and fiscal reports to the state;</p>
        <p>(3) receive state adult basic education aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.531">124D.531</a> for adult basic education programming delivered by the consortium; and</p>
        <p>(4) distribute state adult basic education aid to members of the consortium according to the consortium agreement.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.521.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.1.16.0">2000 c 489 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.522">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.522 ADULT BASIC EDUCATION SUPPLEMENTAL SERVICE GRANTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner, in consultation with the policy review task force under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.521">124D.521</a>, may make grants to nonprofit organizations to provide services that are not offered by a district adult basic education program or that are supplemental to either the statewide adult basic education program, or a district's adult basic education program. The commissioner may make grants for: staff development for adult basic education teachers and administrators; training for volunteer tutors; training, services, and materials for serving disabled students through adult basic education programs; statewide promotion of adult basic education services and programs; development and dissemination of instructional and administrative technology for adult basic education programs; programs which primarily serve communities of color; adult basic education distance learning projects, including television instruction programs; initiatives to accelerate English language acquisition and the achievement of career and college ready skills among English learners; and other supplemental services to support the mission of adult basic education and innovative delivery of adult basic education services.</p>
        <p>(b) The commissioner must establish eligibility criteria and grant application procedures. Grants under this section must support services throughout the state, focus on educational results for adult learners, and promote outcome-based achievement through adult basic education programs. Beginning in fiscal year 2002, the commissioner may make grants under this section from the state total adult basic education aid set aside for supplemental service grants under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.531">124D.531</a>. Up to one-fourth of the appropriation for supplemental service grants must be used for grants for adult basic education programs to encourage and support innovations in adult basic education instruction and service delivery. A grant to a single organization cannot exceed 40 percent of the total supplemental services aid. Nothing in this section prevents an approved adult basic education program from using state or federal aid to purchase supplemental services.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.522.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.1.17.0">2000 c 489 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/3/#laws.3.3.0">1Sp2001 c 3 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.6.8.0">2009 c 96 art 6 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.1.35.0">2014 c 272 art 1 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.20.13.0">2014 c 312 art 20 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.124D.53"><b>124D.53</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.1.46.0">2000 c 489 art 1 s 46</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.531">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.531 ADULT BASIC EDUCATION AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">State total adult basic education aid.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The state total adult basic education aid for fiscal year 2024 equals $52,759,000, plus any amount that is not paid during the previous fiscal year as a result of adjustments under subdivision 4, paragraph (a), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52#stat.124D.52.3">124D.52, subdivision 3</a>. The state total adult basic education aid for later fiscal years equals:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the state total adult basic education aid for the preceding fiscal year plus any amount that is not paid for during the previous fiscal year, as a result of adjustments under subdivision 4, paragraph (a), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52#stat.124D.52.3">124D.52, subdivision 3</a>; times</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the lesser of:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.03">1.03</a>; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the greater of: (A) one plus the percent change in the formula allowance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.2">126C.10, subdivision 2</a>, from the previous fiscal year to the current fiscal year; or (B) the average growth in state total contact hours over the prior ten program years.</p>
          <p class="in">Three percent of the state total adult basic education aid must be set aside for adult basic education supplemental service grants under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.522">124D.522</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The state total adult basic education aid, excluding basic population aid, equals the difference between the amount computed in paragraph (a), and the state total basic population aid under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Basic population aid.</span></h2>
          <p>A district is eligible for basic population aid if the district has a basic service level approved by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52#stat.124D.52.5">124D.52, subdivision 5</a>, or is a member of a consortium with an approved basic service level. Basic population aid is equal to the greater of $3,844 or $1.73 times the population of the district. District population is determined according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.14">275.14</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Program revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>Adult basic education programs established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52">124D.52</a> and approved by the commissioner are eligible for revenue under this subdivision. For fiscal year 2001 and later, adult basic education revenue for each approved program equals the sum of:</p>
          <p>(1) the basic population aid under subdivision 2 for districts participating in the program during the current program year; plus</p>
          <p>(2) 84 percent times the amount computed in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), times the ratio of the contact hours for students participating in the program during the first prior program year to the state total contact hours during the first prior program year; plus</p>
          <p>(3) eight percent times the amount computed in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), times the ratio of the enrollment of English learners during the second prior school year in districts participating in the program during the current program year to the state total enrollment of English learners during the second prior school year in districts participating in adult basic education programs during the current program year; plus</p>
          <p>(4) eight percent times the amount computed in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), times the ratio of the latest federal census count of the number of adults aged 25 or older with no diploma residing in the districts participating in the program during the current program year to the latest federal census count of the state total number of adults aged 25 or older with no diploma residing in the districts participating in adult basic education programs during the current program year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Adult basic education program aid limit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Notwithstanding subdivisions 2 and 3, the total adult basic education aid for a program per prior year contact hour must not exceed $30 per prior year contact hour computed under subdivision 3, clause (2).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The aid for a program under subdivision 3, clause (2), adjusted for changes in program membership, must not exceed the aid for that program under subdivision 3, clause (2), for the first preceding fiscal year by more than the greater of 11 percent or $10,000.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Adult basic education aid is payable to a program for unreimbursed costs occurring in the program year as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52#stat.124D.52.3">124D.52, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Any adult basic education aid that is not paid to a program because of the program aid limitation under paragraph (a) must be added to the state total adult basic education aid for the next fiscal year under subdivision 1. Any adult basic education aid that is not paid to a program because of the program aid limitations under paragraph (b) must be reallocated among programs by adjusting the rate per contact hour under subdivision 3, clause (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.9.19.0">2007 c 146 art 9 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Payment of aid to fiscal agent.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), adult basic education aid must be paid directly to the fiscal agent of each approved program. An approved program must have only one fiscal agent.</p>
          <p>(b) A district that is part of a consortium may request direct payment of basic population aid under subdivision 2. The district must make a written request to the commissioner by June 15 for aid payments the following fiscal year. The request must include certification that:</p>
          <p>(1) the district will deposit direct aid payments in a separate adult basic education account; and</p>
          <p>(2) the district will use direct aid payments only for adult basic education instruction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Program audits.</span></h2>
          <p>Programs that receive aid under this section must maintain records that support the aid payments. The commissioner may audit these records upon request. The commissioner must establish procedures for conducting fiscal audits of adult basic education programs. The commissioner must establish procedures to reconcile any discrepancies between aid payments based on information reported to the commissioner and aid estimates based on a program audit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Administrative cap.</span></h2>
          <p>A consortium or district shall not spend more than five percent of the consortium or district's total adult basic education aid on administrative costs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Fiscal reports.</span></h2>
          <p>Programs that receive aid under this section must submit an annual report to the commissioner that includes revenue and expense reports for each district and program, including instructional services offered in partnership with businesses and nonprofit organizations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.531.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Contact hours in cases of disruption of services.</span></h2>
          <p>An adult basic education program or consortium that has been subject to a service disruption may apply to the commissioner in the form and manner established by the commissioner for an adjusted number of contact hours. The program or consortium must demonstrate to the commissioner's satisfaction that the loss in contact hours due to the service disruption was outside of the control of the adult basic education program or its consortium and that the program or consortium took reasonable actions to avoid the loss of contact hours. If the commissioner approves the program or consortium's request, the commissioner may adjust the number of contact hours of the program, and if applicable, of the consortium, but in no case may the adjusted contact hours yield an aid amount for a consortium under subdivision 3, clause (2), greater than the most recent two-year average aid under that clause.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.531.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.1.19.0">2000 c 489 art 1 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/3/#laws.3.4.0">1Sp2001 c 3 art 3 s 4</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.9.3.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 9 s 3</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.9.2.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 9 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/282/#laws.2.12.0">2006 c 282 art 2 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.9.15.0">2007 c 146 art 9 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/363/#laws.2.14.0">2008 c 363 art 2 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.9.1.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 9 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/145/#laws.0.3.0">2012 c 145 s 3; </a><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.8.3.0">2013 c 116 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.20.14.0">2014 c 312 art 20 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.11.4.0">2023 c 55 art 11 s 4</a>,5</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.124D.54"><b>124D.54</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.9.10.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 9 s 10</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.124D.548"><b>124D.548</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.6.38.0">1998 c 398 art 6 s 38</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.549">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.549 COMMISSIONER-SELECTED HIGH SCHOOL EQUIVALENCY TEST.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner, in consultation with adult basic education stakeholders, must select a high school equivalency test. The commissioner may issue a high school equivalency diploma to a Minnesota resident 19 years of age or older who has not earned a high school diploma, who has not previously been issued a general education development (GED) certification, and who has exceeded or achieved a minimum passing score on the equivalency test established by the publisher. The commissioner of education may waive the minimum age requirement if supportive evidence is provided by an employer or a recognized education or rehabilitation provider.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.549.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.2.16.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1129/#laws.8.16.0">1969 c 1129 art 8 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/305/#laws.0.41.0">1977 c 305 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/764/#laws.0.8.0">1978 c 764 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/150/#laws.0.1.0">1983 c 150 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.8.1.0">1992 c 499 art 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.9.16.0">1993 c 224 art 9 s 16</a>,17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.6.4.0">1998 c 398 art 6 s 4</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.10.2.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 10 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.55">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.55 COMMISSIONER-SELECTED HIGH SCHOOL EQUIVALENCY TEST FEES.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall pay 60 percent of the fee that is charged to an eligible individual for the full battery of the commissioner-selected high school equivalency tests, but not more than $40 for an eligible individual.</p>
        <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), for fiscal years 2023 through 2027 only, subject to the availability of funds, the commissioner shall pay 100 percent of the fee charged to an eligible individual for the full battery of the commissioner-selected high school equivalency tests, but not more than the cost of one full battery of tests per year for any individual.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.55.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.4.11.0">1991 c 265 art 4 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.4.7.0">1992 c 499 art 4 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.3.73.0">1998 c 397 art 3 s 73</a>,103; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/363/#laws.2.15.0">2008 c 363 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.32.3.0">2016 c 189 art 32 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.10.3.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 10 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.9.1.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 9 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.11.6.0">2023 c 55 art 11 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.56">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.56 COMMUNITY EDUCATION PROGRAM REVENUE; ADULTS WITH DISABILITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Revenue amount.</span></h2>
          <p>A district that is eligible according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.20#stat.124D.20.2">124D.20, subdivision 2</a>, may receive revenue for a program for adults with disabilities. Revenue for the program for adults with disabilities for a district equals the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) the actual expenditures for approved programs and budgets; or</p>
          <p>(2) the greater of (i) $0.34 times the population of the school district as determined according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.14">275.14</a>, or (ii) the district's adults with disabilities revenue for fiscal year 2023. If the district does not levy the entire amount permitted, the district's adults with disabilities aid is reduced in proportion to the actual amount levied.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Aid.</span></h2>
          <p>Program aid for adults with disabilities equals the difference between the district's adults with disabilities revenue and the district's adults with disabilities levy.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Levy.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy for a program for adults with disabilities in an amount not to exceed the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) the district's revenue under subdivision 1; or</p>
          <p>(2) the product of a tax rate not to exceed .006 percent in fiscal year 2025, .0053 percent in fiscal year 2026, and .005 percent in fiscal year 2027 and later, times the district's adjusted net tax capacity for the year prior to the year the levy is certified.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Outside revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may receive money from public or private sources to supplement revenue for the program for adults with disabilities. Aid may not be reduced as a result of receiving money from these sources.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Use of revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>Revenue for the program for adults with disabilities may be used only to provide programs for adults with disabilities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.56.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Cooperation encouraged.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district offering programming for adults with disabilities is encouraged to provide programming in cooperation with other school districts and other public and private organizations providing services to adults with disabilities.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.56.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.4.14.0">1989 c 329 art 4 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.3.13.0">1990 c 562 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.3.79.0">1998 c 397 art 3 s 79</a>,103; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.11.7.0">2023 c 55 art 11 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.57">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.57 EDUCATIONAL SUPPORT SERVICES FOR PEOPLE WHO ARE DEAF, DEAFBLIND, HARD-OF-HEARING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.57.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Responsibility of commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall coordinate and may pay for support services for persons who are deaf, deafblind, and hard-of-hearing to assure access to educational opportunities. Services may be provided to adult students who are deaf, deafblind, and hard-of-hearing and (1) have been denied access to educational opportunities because of the lack of support services or (2) are presently enrolled or (3) are contemplating enrollment in an educational program and would benefit from support services. The commissioner shall also be responsible for conducting in-service training for public and private agencies regarding the needs of persons who are deaf, deafblind, and hard-of-hearing in the adult education system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.57.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Support services.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may pay school districts or public or private community agencies for the following support services:</p>
          <p>(1) interpreter services to provide translation for an individual or a group of students; or</p>
          <p>(2) notetaker services to convert spoken language to written language when the student must maintain visual contact with other persons such as an interpreter or instructor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.57.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Programs included.</span></h2>
          <p>Support services may be provided for:</p>
          <p>(1) local school district adult education programs;</p>
          <p>(2) adult technical college programs; and</p>
          <p>(3) avocational education programs sponsored by public or private community agencies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.57.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.3.9.0">1981 c 358 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/258/#laws.0.12.0">1987 c 258 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/246/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 246 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.13.5.0">1993 c 224 art 13 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.3.2.0">1998 c 397 art 3 s 2</a>,103; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.55.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/62/#laws.0.2.0">2013 c 62 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ENGLISH LEARNER</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.58">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.58 CITATION; EDUCATION FOR ENGLISH LEARNERS ACT.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.64">124D.64</a> may be cited as the "Education for English Learners Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.58.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.14.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.164.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 164</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.59">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.59 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65">124D.65</a>, the terms defined in this section shall have the meanings given them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">English learner.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "English learner" means a pupil in kindergarten through grade 12; an early childhood special education student under Part B, section 619, of the Individuals with Disabilities Education Act, United States Code, title 20, section 1419; or a prekindergarten student enrolled in an approved voluntary prekindergarten program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142D.08">142D.08 </a>or a school readiness plus program who meets the requirements under subdivision 2a or the following requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) the pupil, as declared by a parent or guardian first learned a language other than English, comes from a home where the language usually spoken is other than English, or usually speaks a language other than English; and</p>
          <p>(2) the pupil is determined by a valid assessment measuring the pupil's English language proficiency and by developmentally appropriate measures, which might include observations, teacher judgment, parent recommendations, or developmentally appropriate assessment instruments, to lack the necessary English skills to participate fully in academic classes taught in English.</p>
          <p>(b) A pupil enrolled in a Minnesota public school in any grade 4 through 12 who in the previous school year took a commissioner-provided assessment measuring the pupil's emerging academic English, shall be counted as an English learner in calculating English learner pupil units under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05#stat.126C.05.17">126C.05, subdivision 17</a>, and shall generate state English learner aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65#stat.124D.65.5">124D.65, subdivision 5</a>, if the pupil scored below the state cutoff score or is otherwise counted as a nonproficient participant on the assessment measuring the pupil's emerging academic English, or, in the judgment of the pupil's classroom teachers, consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.61">124D.61</a>, clause (1), the pupil is unable to demonstrate academic language proficiency in English, including oral academic language, sufficient to successfully and fully participate in the general core curriculum in the regular classroom.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) and (b), a pupil in early childhood special education or prekindergarten under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142D.08">142D.08</a>, through grade 12 shall not be counted as an English learner in calculating English learner pupil units under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05#stat.126C.05.17">126C.05, subdivision 17</a>, and shall not generate state English learner aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65#stat.124D.65.5">124D.65, subdivision 5</a>, if:</p>
          <p>(1) the pupil is not enrolled during the current fiscal year in an educational program for English learners under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.64">124D.64</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) the pupil has generated seven or more years of average daily membership in Minnesota public schools since July 1, 1996.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">English learner; limited or interrupted formal education.</span></h2>
          <p>Consistent with subdivision 2, an English learner with limited or interrupted formal education is an English learner under subdivision 2 who has at least two fewer years of schooling than the English learner's peers when entering school in the United States.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Essential instructional personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>"Essential instructional personnel" means the following:</p>
          <p>(1) a teacher licensed by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board to teach bilingual education or English as a second language;</p>
          <p>(2) any teacher as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.15">122A.15</a> who holds a valid license from the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board, if the district assures the department that the teacher will obtain the preservice and in-service training the department considers necessary to enable the teacher to provide appropriate service to English learners.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">English as a second language program.</span></h2>
          <p>"English as a second language program" means a program for the instruction of English learners in the following English language skills: reading, writing, listening and speaking.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Bilingual education program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Bilingual education program" means an educational program in which instruction is given in both English and the primary language of the English learner to the extent necessary to allow the pupil to progress effectively through the educational system and to attain the basic skills of reading, writing, listening, and speaking in the English language so that the pupil will be able to perform ordinary classwork successfully in English.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Primary language.</span></h2>
          <p>"Primary language" means a language other than English which is the language normally used by the child or the language which is spoken in the child's home environment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Parent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Parent" includes a child's legal guardian.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Educational program for English learners.</span></h2>
          <p>"Educational program for English learners" means an English as a second language program, bilingual education program, or both an English as a second language and a bilingual education program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.59.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">English learner data.</span></h2>
          <p>When data on English learners are reported for purposes of educational accountability, English learner data must include all pupils enrolled in a Minnesota public school course or program who are currently or were previously counted as an English learner under this section. Reported data must be disaggregated by currently counted and previously counted English learners.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.59.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.15.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.3.18.0">1981 c 358 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.3.22.0">1982 c 548 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.130.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 130</a>,131,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.2.48.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 2 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.13.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/277/#laws.1.10.0">2008 c 277 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.13.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.1.36.0">2014 c 272 art 1 s 36</a>,37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.15.6.0">2014 c 312 art 15 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/3/#laws.1.4.0">1Sp2015 c 3 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.25.35.0">2016 c 189 art 25 s 35</a>; art 27 s 7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.8.7.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 8 s 7</a>; art 12 s 22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.1.8.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.1.7.0">2023 c 55 art 1 s 7</a>; art 2 s 53; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.4.26.0">2024 c 80 art 4 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.42.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 42</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.60">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.60 RIGHTS OF PARENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.60.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Within 30 calendar days after the beginning of the school year, the district or charter school in which the English learner identified for participation in an instructional program for English learners is enrolled must notify the parents. For those children who have not been identified as English learners prior to the beginning of the school year but are identified as English learners during that school year, the district or charter school shall notify the children's parents during the first two weeks of the child being placed in a language instruction educational program. This notice must:</p>
          <p>(1) be in writing in English and in the primary language of the pupil's parents;</p>
          <p>(2) inform the parents that their child has been enrolled in an instructional program for English learners;</p>
          <p>(3) contain a simple, nontechnical description of the purposes, method and content of the program;</p>
          <p>(4) inform the parents that they have the right to visit the educational program for English learners in which their child is enrolled;</p>
          <p>(5) inform the parents of the time and manner in which to request and receive a conference for the purpose of explaining the nature and purpose of the program; and</p>
          <p>(6) inform the parents of their rights to withdraw their child from an educational program for English learners and the time and manner in which to do so.</p>
          <p>The department shall, at the request of the district, prepare the notice in the primary language of the parent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.60.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Withdrawal from program.</span></h2>
          <p>Any parent whose child is enrolled in an educational program for English learners shall have the right, either at the time of the original notification of enrollment or at the close of any semester thereafter, to withdraw the child from the program by providing written notice of this intent to the principal of the school in which the child is enrolled or to the superintendent of the district in which the child resides. Nothing in this section shall preclude a parent from reenrolling the child in an educational program for English learners.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.60.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Parental involvement.</span></h2>
          <p>A district which receives moneys pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65">124D.65</a> must encourage involvement of parents of pupils enrolled in the educational program for English learners in this program. The district shall solicit the views of parents about the program and its effects upon their children.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.60.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.17.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.3.23.0">1982 c 548 art 3 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.132.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 132</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.2.28.0">2024 c 109 art 2 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.61">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.61 GENERAL REQUIREMENTS FOR PROGRAMS.</h1>
        <p>A district that enrolls one or more English learners must implement an educational program that includes at a minimum the following requirements:</p>
        <p>(1) identification and reclassification criteria for English learners and program entrance and exit criteria for English learners must be documented by the district, applied uniformly to English learners, and made available to parents and other stakeholders upon request;</p>
        <p>(2) language development instruction that is designed to effectively increase the language proficiency of English learners and that addresses Minnesota's English language development standards under Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/3501.1200">3501.1200</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/3501.1210">3501.1210</a>;</p>
        <p>(3) a written plan of services that describes programming by English proficiency level made available to parents upon request. The plan must articulate the amount and scope of service offered to English learners through an educational program for English learners;</p>
        <p>(4) professional development opportunities for ESL, bilingual education, mainstream, and all staff working with English learners which are: (i) coordinated with the district's professional development activities; (ii) related to the needs of English learners; and (iii) ongoing;</p>
        <p>(5) to the extent possible, avoid isolating English learners for a substantial part of the school day; and</p>
        <p>(6) in predominantly nonverbal subjects, such as art, music, and physical education, permit English learners to participate fully and on an equal basis with their contemporaries in public school classes provided for these subjects. To the extent possible, the district must assure to pupils enrolled in a program for English learners an equal and meaningful opportunity to participate fully with other pupils in all extracurricular activities.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.61.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.18.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.3.24.0">1982 c 548 art 3 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.133.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 133</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/254/#laws.0.31.0">2000 c 254 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.2.17.0">2006 c 263 art 2 s 17; 2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.2.29.0">2024 c 109 art 2 s 29</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.124D.62"><b>124D.62</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.2.48.0">2007 c 146 art 2 s 48</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.63">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.63 TECHNICAL ASSISTANCE.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall provide technical assistance to districts receiving aid pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65">124D.65</a> and to postsecondary institutions for preservice and in-service training for bilingual education teachers and English as a second language teachers employed in educational programs for English learners, teaching methods, curriculum development, testing and testing mechanisms, and the development of instructional materials for these educational programs.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.63.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.20.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.3.25.0">1982 c 548 art 3 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.13.47.0">1993 c 224 art 13 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.135.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 135</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.64">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.64 DISCRIMINATION PROHIBITED.</h1>
        <p>Nothing in the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.63">124D.63</a> shall be construed to violate the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.30">123B.30</a> or chapter 363A. Programs and activities pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.63">124D.63</a> shall be deemed to be positive action programs to combat discrimination.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.64.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.22.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.14.16.0">1993 c 224 art 14 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.2.1.0">1994 c 465 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.164.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 164</a>; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.645">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.645 MULTIRACIAL DIVERSITY.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) Notwithstanding other law or rule to the contrary and in order to effectively meet students' educational needs and foster parents' meaningful participation in their children's education, a school district may apply to the commissioner for a waiver from the requirement to maintain racial balance within a district school if the racial imbalance in that school results from:</p>
        <p class="in">(1) the enrollment of protected multiracial students and the proportion of enrolled multiracial students reflects the proportion of multiracial students who reside in the school attendance area or who are enrolled in the grade levels served by the district; or</p>
        <p class="in">(2) the enrollment of English learners in a transition program that includes an intensive English component.</p>
        <p class="bl">The commissioner must grant the waiver if the district in which the school is located offers the multiracial students or the English learners, as appropriate, the option of enrolling in another school with the requisite racial balance, and the students' parents choose not to pursue that option.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) This section is effective for the 2006-2007 through 2010-2011 school years or until amended rules are adopted under Minnesota Rules, chapter 3535, pertaining to racial diversity, whichever comes first.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.645.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.2.26.0">2007 c 146 art 2 s 26; 2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.65">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.65 ENGLISH LEARNER (EL) PROGRAMS AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.1.69.0">1999 c 241 art 1 s 69</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.1.69.0">1999 c 241 art 1 s 69</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.1.69.0">1999 c 241 art 1 s 69</a>; art 2 s 62]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.54.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">School district EL revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For fiscal year 2024 through fiscal year 2026, a district's English learner programs revenue equals the sum of:</p>
          <p>(1) the product of (i) $1,228, and (ii) the greater of 20 or the adjusted average daily membership of eligible English learners enrolled in the district during the current fiscal year; and</p>
          <p>(2) $436 times the English learner pupil units under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05#stat.126C.05.17">126C.05, subdivision 17</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For fiscal year 2027 and later, a district's English learner programs revenue equals the sum of:</p>
          <p>(1) the product of (i) $1,775, and (ii) the greater of 20 or the adjusted average daily membership of eligible English learners enrolled in the district during the current fiscal year; and</p>
          <p>(2) $630 times the English learner pupil units under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05#stat.126C.05.17">126C.05, subdivision 17</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) A pupil ceases to generate state English learner aid in the school year following the school year in which the pupil attains the state cutoff score on a commissioner-provided assessment that measures the pupil's emerging academic English.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">English learner cross subsidy aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For fiscal year 2027 and later, a district's English learner cross subsidy aid equals 25 percent of the district's English learner cross subsidy calculated under paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(b) A district's English learner cross subsidy equals the greater of zero or the difference between the district's expenditures for qualifying English learner services for the second previous year and the district's English learner revenue under subdivision 5 for the second previous year. For the purposes of this subdivision, "qualifying English learner services" means the services necessary to implement the language instruction educational program for students identified as English learners under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.65">124D.65</a>. Only expenditures that both address the English language development standards in Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/3501.1200">3501.1200</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/3501.1210">3501.1210</a>, which may include home language instruction, and are supplemental to the cost of core content instruction may be included as expenditures for qualifying English learner services. Expenditures do not include costs related to construction, indirect costs, core content instruction, or core administrative personnel.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Participation of nonpublic school pupils.</span></h2>
          <p>In counting the number of English learners for purposes of this section, districts may include English learners who attend nonpublic schools in the district. A district which counts those pupils and receives aid pursuant to this section must offer those pupils the same programs on the same terms that it offers to English learners who attend the public school. A program provided for a nonpublic school pupil pursuant to this subdivision must be provided at a public school or a neutral site as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.41#stat.123B.41.13">123B.41, subdivision 13</a>. Nonpublic school pupils served by a district's educational program for English learners must be counted for average daily membership pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.01">126C.01</a>, subdivisions 6 to 8, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.19">126C.19</a>, subdivisions 1 to 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Application dates.</span></h2>
          <p>To obtain aid for English learner programs, a district must submit information required by the department to implement this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Notification; revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>The department must promptly inform each applicant district of the amount of revenue it will receive pursuant to this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Records; audit.</span></h2>
          <p>A district which applies for aid pursuant to this section must maintain records which support the information contained in all of its applications. The commissioner may audit these records upon request. A district which receives aid pursuant to this section must keep additional records in the manner prescribed by the commissioner to ensure that an educational program for English learners is implemented and operated in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.58">124D.58</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.64">124D.64</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Money from other sources.</span></h2>
          <p>A district providing a program for English learners must be eligible to receive moneys for these programs from other government agencies and from private sources when these moneys are available.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.65.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Allocations from cooperative units.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For the purposes of this section, English learners enrolled in a cooperative or intermediate school district unit shall be counted by the school district of residence, and the cooperative unit shall allocate its approved expenditures for English learner programs among participating school districts. English learner aid for services provided by a cooperative or intermediate school district shall be paid to the participating school districts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.65.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.3.10.0">1981 c 358 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.3.8.0">1982 c 548 art 3 s 8</a>-11; art 7 s 26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.3.4.0">1989 c 329 art 3 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.3.5.0">1991 c 265 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.3.11.0">1993 c 224 art 3 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.2.5.0">2000 c 489 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.3.4.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 3 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.14.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 14</a>;       <h1 class="fgh">ASSURANCE OF MASTERY</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.66">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.66 ASSURANCE OF MASTERY PROGRAMS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.66.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligible districts.</span></h2>
          <p>A district with a local process to review curriculum and instruction may provide an assurance of mastery program to eligible pupils.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.66.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligible pupils.</span></h2>
          <p>A pupil is eligible to receive services through an assurance of mastery program if the pupil has not demonstrated progress toward mastering the required graduation standards, after receiving instruction that was designed to enable the pupil to make progress toward mastering the required graduation standards in a regular classroom setting. A pupil also is eligible to receive services through an assurance of mastery program if the pupil, based on the professional judgment of a classroom teacher or a team of licensed professionals, demonstrates a need for alternative instructional strategies or interventions. To determine pupil eligibility, a district must use a process adopted by the school board to review curriculum and instruction, for the subjects and at the grade level at which the district uses the revenue.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.66.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Eligible services.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Assurance of mastery programs may provide direct scientific, research-based instructional services and intervention to an eligible pupil, or a group of eligible pupils, under the following conditions in paragraphs (b) to (d).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Instruction may be provided at one or more grade levels from kindergarten to grade 8 and for students in grades 9 through 12 who were enrolled in grade 8 before the 2005-2006 school year and have failed the basic skills tests, or were enrolled in grade 8 in the 2005-2006 school year and later and who have failed the Minnesota Comprehensive Assessments (MCA-IIs) in reading, mathematics, or writing as required for high school graduation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.02">120B.02</a>. If an assessment of pupils' needs within a district demonstrates that the eligible pupils in grades kindergarten to grade 8 are being appropriately served, a district may serve eligible pupils in grades 9 to 12.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Instruction must be provided under the supervision of the eligible pupil's regular classroom teacher. Instruction may be provided by the eligible pupil's classroom teacher, by another teacher, by a team of teachers, or by an education assistant or aide. A special education teacher may provide instruction, but instruction that is provided under this section is not eligible for aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The instruction that is provided must differ from the initial instruction the pupil received in the regular classroom setting. The instruction may differ by presenting different curriculum than was initially presented in the regular classroom or by presenting the same curriculum:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) at a different rate or in a different sequence than it was initially presented;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) using different teaching methods or techniques than were used initially; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) using different instructional materials than were used initially.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.66.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/4/#laws.2.12.0">1Sp1997 c 4 art 2 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.164.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 164</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.2.25.0">1998 c 398 art 2 s 25</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.2.67.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 2 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.2.27.0">2007 c 146 art 2 s 27</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.124D.67"><b>124D.67</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.1.69.0">1999 c 241 art 1 s 69</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">GRADUATION INCENTIVES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.68">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.68 GRADUATION INCENTIVES PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>The legislature finds that it is critical to provide options for children to succeed in school. Therefore, the purpose of this section is to provide incentives for and encourage all Minnesota students who have experienced or are experiencing difficulty in the traditional education system to enroll in alternative programs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligible pupils.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A pupil under the age of 21 or who meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.20">120A.20</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (c), and a pupil with a disability until the pupil is 22 years of age, is eligible to participate in the graduation incentives program, if the pupil:</p>
          <p>(1) performs substantially below the performance level for pupils of the same age in a locally determined achievement test;</p>
          <p>(2) is behind in satisfactorily completing coursework or obtaining credits for graduation;</p>
          <p>(3) is pregnant or is a parent;</p>
          <p>(4) has been assessed as having substance use disorder;</p>
          <p>(5) has been excluded or expelled according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.40">121A.40</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.56">121A.56</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) has been referred by a school district for enrollment in an eligible program or a program pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.69">124D.69</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) is a victim of physical or sexual abuse;</p>
          <p>(8) has experienced mental health problems;</p>
          <p>(9) has experienced homelessness sometime within six months before requesting a transfer to an eligible program;</p>
          <p>(10) speaks English as a second language or is an English learner;</p>
          <p>(11) has withdrawn from school or has been chronically truant; or</p>
          <p>(12) is being treated in a hospital in the seven-county metropolitan area for cancer or other life threatening illness or is the sibling of an eligible pupil who is being currently treated, and resides with the pupil's family at least 60 miles beyond the outside boundary of the seven-county metropolitan area.</p>
          <p>(b) A pupil is eligible to participate in the graduation incentives program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.68">124D.68</a> and in concurrent enrollment courses offered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.09#stat.124D.09.10">124D.09, subdivision 10</a>, and is funded in the same manner as other pupils under this section if the pupil otherwise qualifies under paragraph (a), is at least 21 years of age and not yet 22 years of age, and:</p>
          <p>(1) is an English learner with a limited or interrupted formal education according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.59#stat.124D.59.2a">124D.59, subdivision 2a</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) meets three of the following four requirements:</p>
          <p>(i) comes from a home where the language usually spoken is other than English, or usually speaks a language other than English;</p>
          <p>(ii) enters school in the United States after grade 6;</p>
          <p>(iii) functions at least two years below expected grade level in reading and mathematics; and</p>
          <p>(iv) may be preliterate in the English learner's native language.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Eligible programs.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A pupil who is eligible according to subdivision 2 may enroll in a state-approved alternative program under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.05">123A.05</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.08">123A.08</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A pupil who is eligible according to subdivision 2 and who is a high school junior or senior may enroll in postsecondary courses under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.09">124D.09</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) A pupil who is eligible under subdivision 2, may enroll in any public elementary or secondary education program.</p>
          <p>(d) A pupil who is eligible under subdivision 2, may enroll in any nonpublic, nonsectarian school that has contracted with the serving school district to provide educational services. However, notwithstanding other provisions of this section, only a pupil who is eligible under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (12), may enroll in a contract alternative school that is specifically structured to provide educational services to such a pupil.</p>
          <p>(e) A pupil who is between the ages of 17 and 21 may enroll in any adult basic education programs approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52">124D.52</a> and operated under the community education program contained in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.19">124D.19</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Additional eligible program.</span></h2>
          <p>A pupil who is at least 16 years of age, who is eligible under subdivision 2, and who has been enrolled only in a public school, if the pupil has been enrolled in any school, during the year immediately before transferring under this subdivision, may transfer to any nonpublic school that has contracted with the serving school district to provide nonsectarian educational services. The school must enroll every eligible pupil who seeks to transfer to the school under this program subject to available space.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Pupil enrollment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any eligible pupil may apply to enroll in an eligible program. Approval of the resident district is not required for:</p>
          <p>(1) an eligible pupil to enroll in any eligible program in a nonresident district under subdivision 3 or 4 or a state-approved alternative program established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.05">123A.05</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) an eligible pupil under subdivision 2, to enroll in an adult basic education program approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52">124D.52</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), a nonresident district must first approve the enrollment application of any eligible pupil who was expelled under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.45">121A.45</a> for a reason stated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.03#stat.124D.03.1">124D.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Dissemination of information.</span></h2>
          <p>A district must disseminate information, developed by the department, about the graduation incentives program to residents in the district who are under the age of 21.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Desegregation plans.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision to the contrary, students may not enroll in a nonresident district under this section if their enrollment in another district would result in a violation of a district's desegregation plan, as mandated and approved by the commissioner of education.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Aid adjustments.</span></h2>
          <p>General education aid and transportation aid attributable to a pupil covered by programs under this section must be paid according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.3">123B.92, subdivision 3</a>, respectively.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Enrollment verification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For a pupil attending an eligible program full time under subdivision 3, paragraph (d), the department must pay 90 percent of the district's average general education revenue less basic skills revenue to the eligible program and ten percent of the district's average general education revenue less basic skills revenue to the contracting district within 30 days after the eligible program verifies enrollment using the form provided by the department. For a pupil attending an eligible program part time, revenue, excluding compensatory revenue, shall be reduced proportionately, according to the amount of time the pupil attends the program, and the payments to the eligible program and the contracting district shall be reduced accordingly. A pupil for whom payment is made according to this section may not be counted by any district for any purpose other than computation of general education revenue. If payment is made for a pupil under this subdivision, a district shall not reimburse a program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.69">124D.69</a> for the same pupil. The basic skills revenue generated by pupils attending the eligible program according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.4">126C.10, subdivision 4</a>, shall be paid to the eligible program.</p>
          <p>(b) The department must pay up to 100 percent of the revenue to the eligible program if there is an agreement to that effect between the school district and the eligible program.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) and (b), for an eligible program that provides chemical treatment services to students, the department must pay 100 percent of the revenue to the eligible program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.68.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Severability.</span></h2>
          <p>If for any reason any portion of this section is found by a court to be unconstitutional, the remaining portions of the section shall remain in effect.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.68.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.8.15.0">1987 c 398 art 8 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/718/#laws.6.12.0">1988 c 718 art 6 s 12</a>,13; art 7 s 37-40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/222/#laws.0.30.0">1989 c 222 s 30</a>,31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.7.8.0">1989 c 329 art 7 s 8</a>,9; art 9 s 14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.4.4.0">1990 c 562 art 4 s 4</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.4.20.0">1991 c 265 art 4 s 20</a>-25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/363/#laws.2.3.0">1992 c 363 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.1.30.0">2018 c 182 art 1 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.2.18.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/116/#laws.1.1.0">2020 c 116 art 1 s 1</a>; art 7 s 6; 2024 c 85 s 115</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.69">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.69 AID FOR ALTERNATIVE PROGRAMS PROVIDED UNDER CONTRACT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.69.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Aid.</span></h2>
          <p>If a pupil enrolls in an alternative program, eligible under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.68#stat.124D.68.3">124D.68, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (d), or subdivision 4, operated by a private organization that has contracted with a school district to provide educational services for eligible pupils under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.68#stat.124D.68.2">124D.68, subdivision 2</a>, the district contracting with the private organization must reimburse the provider an amount equal to the sum of (1) at least 95 percent of the district's average general education less basic skills revenue per pupil unit times the number of pupil units for pupils attending the program, and (2) the amount of basic skills revenue generated by pupils attending the program according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.4">126C.10, subdivision 4</a>. For a pupil attending the program part time, the revenue paid to the program, excluding compensatory revenue, must be reduced proportionately, according to the amount of time the pupil attends the program, and revenue paid to the district shall be reduced accordingly. Pupils for whom a district provides reimbursement may not be counted by the district for any purpose other than computation of general education revenue. If payment is made to a district or program for a pupil under this section, the department must not make a payment for the same pupil under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.68#stat.124D.68.9">124D.68, subdivision 9</a>. Notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.15">125A.15</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.51">125A.51</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.515">125A.515</a>, general education revenue for a student who receives educational services under this section shall be paid according to this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.69.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reserve account.</span></h2>
          <p>During the term of the contract to provide educational services under subdivision 1, all state aid under subdivision 1 accrues to the account assigned to the alternative program site and is reserved for that site.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.69.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Uncommon schools serving students with substance use disorder; allocation of funds.</span></h2>
          <p>In addition to the amounts provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.68#stat.124D.68.9">124D.68, subdivision 9</a>, a school district may allocate funds from its undesignated general fund to a private contracted alternative program, including a private contracted alternative program that is tuition free and provides a comprehensive secondary academic program for students who have been assessed as having substance use disorder and who have completed a licensed treatment program for substance use disorder.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.124D.69.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.8.16.0">1987 c 398 art 8 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/486/#laws.0.70.0">1988 c 486 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/718/#laws.7.41.0">1988 c 718 art 7 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.7.10.0">1989 c 329 art 7 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.4.7.0">1990 c 562 art 4 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.7.21.0">1991 c 265 art 7 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.9.13.0">1992 c 499 art 9 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.4.31.0">1994 c 647 art 4 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.4.23.0">1996 c 412 art 4 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/4/#laws.2.36.0">1Sp1997 c 4 art 2 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.127.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 127</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.1.6.0">1999 c 241 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.13.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/374/#laws.4.1.0">2002 c 374 art 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.12.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.4.51.0">2022 c 98 art 4 s 51</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.124D.695">
        <h1 class="shn">124D.695 APPROVED RECOVERY PROGRAM FUNDING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.124D.695.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Approved recovery program.</span></h2>
